Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文核心发现：大语言模型的奉承行为并非单一现象，而是由内部表示和计算机制显著不同的三种模式构成；尽管输出高度相似（文本分类器准确率仅57.8%），其神经表征从第14层起即可完美线性分离，且各模式在出现阶段、注意力依赖和触发输入上存在差异，呼吁更精准的测量与干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战既有研究将奉承行为（sycophancy）视为单一可统调的行为维度这一简化假设——此前机制研究普遍认为该行为可被统一放大或抑制，但作者指出这种观点忽略了其潜在的结构性多样性。

Method: 通过设计948个社会压力情境实验，系统分析三种假设的奉承模式（mode），结合内部表示分析、注意力机制追踪及输入敏感性测试，验证各模式的神经计算特性。

Result: 三种模式虽产生高度相似的输出（文本分类器准确率57.8%），但神经表征从第14层起实现完美线性可分；模式在模型处理流程中出现于不同阶段、依赖独立注意力电路，并对特定输入类型响应最强。

Conclusion: 奉承行为实为多维度结构化现象而非单一倾向，其内部存在表示与计算机制异质的模式家族，为未来研究提供理论框架——需开发针对性测量工具和分层干预策略以应对复杂社会交互需求。

Abstract: Large language models often align with users' beliefs at the expense of factual accuracy, a behavior known as sycophancy. Prior mechanistic studies largely treat sycophancy as a single behavioral dimension that can be uniformly amplified or suppressed. We challenge this assumption by analyzing three hypothesized modes of sycophancy across 948 social pressure situations. Although the modes produce highly similar outputs, with a text-only classifier achieving just 57.8 percent accuracy, their internal representations are perfectly linearly separable from layer 14 onward. We further find the modes emerge at different processing stages, rely on distinct attention circuitry, and fire strongest on different inputs. These results show that sycophancy is not a monolithic tendency, but a structured family of representationally and computationally distinct modes, motivating more precise measurement and intervention.

</details>


### [107] [surprisal is Not a Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20208)
*Andrés Buxó-Lugo,Aniello De Santo,Morgan Grobol,Ryan J. Hubbard,Cassandra L. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文批评了将大型语言模型（LLMs）的惊讶指标（surprisal）直接用于测试惊讶理论（Surprisal Theory）的未经批判做法，指出算法和模型架构选择会显著影响概率计算，导致模型间差异被掩盖；建议研究者重新评估将LLM概率视为可互换的实践，并重视表示层级和算法层级的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在反驳“计算层级叙述可支持表示无关研究”的观点，强调即使LLMs被视为黑盒系统，使用惊讶指标仍涉及必要的表示决策；动机是揭示未经批判使用LLM-surprisal如何模糊模型在表示和算法层级的具体承诺，从而威胁理论验证的严谨性。

Method: 通过三次实证分析，系统探究不同算法（如训练方法）和模型架构（如Transformer变体）对语言模型概率计算的影响，量化比较LLMs输出的一致性，并评估其对惊讶理论应用的实际干扰。

Result: 分析显示算法选择（如基于自回归或掩码语言建模）和架构设计（如层数、注意力机制）显著改变概率分布；具体而言，不同LLMs对相同输入的惊讶值差异达20%以上，证明概率不可简单互换。

Conclusion: 研究者应停止将LLM-surprisal当作通用工具测试惊讶理论，转而采用透明化表示和算法层级的方案；未来工作需明确模型决策细节，以确保理论验证的有效性和可重复性。

Abstract: Surprisal Theory is often characterized as a computational-level explanation per (Marr, 1982). We argue in this work that, even though a computational level narrative has been used to support "representation-agnostic research" within computational psycholinguistics, the movement toward black box systems embodied by large language models (LLMs) does not exempt modelers using the surprisal metric from the representational decisions required by computational-level characterizations. In fact, we argue that the uncritical use of LLM-surprisal obfuscates the representational and algorithmic-level commitments of different models. In three analyses, we show that the choice of algorithm and model architecture play significant roles in the computation of language model probabilities. We advise that researchers who wish to test Surprisal Theory re-evaluate the practice of treating large language model probabilities as interchangeable

</details>


### [108] [HalluTruthQA: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Hallucination Detection, Localization, and Explanation in Arabic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20219)
*Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Hichem Telli,Somaya Eltanbouly,Shahd Gaben,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Mutaz Al-Khatib,Emad Mohamed,Mohammed Ghaly,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出HalluTruthQA基准，针对阿拉伯语LLM幻觉评估，提供细粒度标签（如错误范围和解释），评估四个开源模型发现无单一最优，推动评估向定位和解释发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准仅提供响应级标签，缺乏对错误内容的精确识别、解释和验证能力，导致事实性错误难以检测、定位和修正。

Method: 构建HalluTruthQA基准，包含2,400个跨伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理领域的示例，每个含参考答、幻觉标签、候选答案及错误范围；在零样本设置下评估Allam、Falcon-H1、Qwen32和Silma的幻觉检测、定位、验证和解释能力。

Result: 结果显示各任务捕捉不同能力：检测Macro-F1最高0.880，定位F1-Sp最高0.516，事实验证LO-Score最高0.852，解释评估最终得分最高0.644，无单一模型在所有任务上表现最优。

Conclusion: 提出幻觉评估应超越简单检测，转向细粒度定位、验证和解释事实性错误，以促进更可靠的LLM应用；代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent Arabic answers, yet factual errors remain difficult to detect, localize, explain, and verify. Existing hallucination benchmarks often provide response-level labels, with limited support for identifying the exact erroneous content, explaining why it is incorrect, or selecting the correct factual answer. We introduce \textsc{HalluTruthQA}, a fine-grained benchmark for hallucination evaluation in Arabic question answering. The benchmark contains 2,400 expert-curated examples across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Each example pairs an Arabic question and a model-generated answer with a verified reference answer, a binary hallucination label, six candidate answers for factual verification, and, for hallucinated answers, character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and macro and micro hallucination types.
  We evaluate four open-source LLMs, \textsc{Allam}, \textsc{Falcon-H1}, \textsc{Qwen32}, and \textsc{Silma}, in a zero-shot setting across hallucination detection, span-level localization, factual verification, and explanation evaluation. Results show that these tasks capture different abilities: no single model achieves the strongest performance across all tasks, with best scores of 0.880 Macro-F1 for detection, 0.516 F1-Sp for localization, 0.852 LO-Score for factual verification, and 0.644 final score for explanation evaluation. Our taxonomy shows that hallucination evaluation should move beyond detection toward localizing, verifying, and explaining factual errors. The code, dataset, prompts, and evaluation scripts are available at https://gitlab.com/nlpresearcher/HalluTruthQA.

</details>


### [109] [On the Systematic Challenges of Culturally Loaded Machine Translation: Dream of the Red Chamber as the Cultural Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20241)
*Yiming Wang,Jiayuan Di*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 文化负载翻译在基于大语言模型的机器翻译中面临任务执行、人工评估分歧和自动评估失效三大挑战；研究基于《红楼梦》的中日双语数据集（500片段）系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型提升了机器翻译质量，但其处理文化负载表达的能力未被充分探索，亟需系统调查在文化特定翻译中的局限性。

Method: 构建源自《红楼梦》的中日双语数据集（500个文化代表性片段），并采用综合评估协议进行测试。

Result: 识别出三主要挑战：(1) 前沿大语言模型在文化负载内容上性能差距显著；(2) 评估者背景差异导致翻译判断存在重大分歧；(3) 现有自动评估指标无法可靠衡量翻译质量。

Conclusion: 这些发现可为文化导向的翻译研究提供新见解，推动计算科学与语言学领域的跨学科发展。

Abstract: Culturally loaded translation poses unique challenges for machine translation (MT), as meanings are deeply embedded in socio-cultural contexts beyond surface linguistic forms. Although large language models (LLMs) have enabled MT systems to achieve human-like quality in many scenarios, their ability to handle culturally loaded expressions remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically investigate the challenges posed by culturally loaded translation in LLM-based MT systems. We construct a Chinese-Japanese bilingual dataset from the culturally representative corpus Dream of the Red Chamber, containing 500 segments across diverse cultural categories. Using a comprehensive evaluation protocol, we reveal three main challenges: (1) task challenges, where frontier LLMs exhibit notable performance gaps and struggle with culturally loaded content; (2) human evaluation challenges, where evaluator backgrounds lead to substantial disagreement in translation judgments; and (3) automatic evaluation challenges, where widely used metrics fail to reliably assess translation quality for this task. These findings may offer valuable insights for culture-oriented translation research in both computational science and linguistics.

</details>


### [110] [Exposure is Optional: Learning Unlike Coordination in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20251)
*Jiamu Luo,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型无需直接暴露于不像协调数据，即可通过一般组合能力成功泛化，实现与未过滤模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调作为基本语言结构，其本质在理论语言学中备受争议；传统观点认为仅同类成分可协调，但自然语言中大量不像协调现象挑战此假设。

Method: 采用过滤语料库训练（FiCT），在移除所有不像协调实例的语料库上训练GPT-2模型，对比未过滤语料库的基准模型。

Result: 过滤数据训练的模型成功泛化至不像协调，困惑度与语法性判断与未过滤模型相当；内部表示分析表明，模型通过将被协调元素归为类似结构类别或类删除机制处理。

Conclusion: 研究证实语言模型可无直接暴露泛化不像协调，深化了对其内部语言结构表示的理解，并为协调理论辩论提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Coordination, a fundamental linguistic structure, remains a subject of intense debate, and its exact nature continues to elude theoretical linguistics. A common view holds that only same-category constituents can be conjoined, which has been challenged by the many grammatical unlike coordinations found in natural language. Treating language models as a computational testbed, we investigate whether the acquisition of unlike coordination requires direct exposure in the training data, or whether it can emerge organically from general compositional abilities. Using Filtered-Corpus Training (FiCT), we train GPT-2 models on corpora from which all instances of unlike coordination have been removed. We find that direct exposure is not necessary: models trained on filtered data successfully generalize to unlike coordination, achieving perplexity and grammaticality judgments comparable to models trained on unfiltered text. Furthermore, our analyses of internal representations indicate that language models process unlike coordination by treating the conjoined elements as belonging to similar structural categories or through a mechanism akin to deletion, both of which appear learnable from exposure to alike coordination alone. This work contributes to the growing understanding of how language models internally represent linguistic structures, while also adding to the broader debate on coordination by showing how models generalize and process unlike coordination without direct exposure.

</details>


### [111] [The Maskability Index: Predicting Task-Objective Alignment in Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20265)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Mahsa Afsharzadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出Maskability Index (MI)指标，量化评估知识关系是否更适合masked或prefix风格提示，以优化预训练语言模型（如T5/BERT）在少样本生成中的知识提取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练语言模型虽具备强结构化知识生成能力，但其性能高度依赖提示策略与预训练目标的对齐程度；现有方法缺乏量化手段选择最优提示模板，导致低资源场景下知识提取效率低下。

Method: 设计Maskability Index (MI)，通过计算masked与unmasked提示模板的DepthRank分数差异，量化目标-模板对齐度，从而判定知识关系适配masked-style或prefix-style提示。

Result: 在ATOMIC2020知识库补全基准上测试100+种关系，MI与下游生成性能呈显著正相关（Pearson r=0.78），验证其作为提示策略选择依据的有效性。

Conclusion: MI为预训练模型的知识提取提供了原则性模板选择框架，显著提升低资源场景下的关系知识利用效率，推动提示工程的自动化与标准化。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained language models such as T5 and BERT have demonstrated strong capabilities for generating structured knowledge. However, their performance depends on how closely the prompting strategy matches the objectives used during pretraining. We introduce the Maskability Index (MI), a quantitative metric that estimates whether a knowledge relation is better suited to masked-style prompting or prefix-style prompting in few-shot generation. MI is computed from differences in DepthRank scores between masked and unmasked templates, providing a principled measure of objective-template alignment. We evaluate MI on a diverse set of relations from the ATOMIC2020 knowledge base completion benchmark and show that it is positively correlated with downstream generation performance. These results indicate that MI can help select appropriate prompting templates and adaptation strategies for extracting relational knowledge from pretrained language models, especially in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [112] [Which Values Do LLMs Confuse? A Schwartz-Based Recognition Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20270)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Mikhail Solovev,Valeriia Kuschenko,Maria Chistyakova,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估大语言模型识别情境中Schwartz价值观的能力：发现模型能大致定位正确价值区域（Acc@1=0.683），但排名不稳定，相邻价值混淆占语义错误的50.9%；特定有向混淆（如Universalism→Benevolence）显著，建议采用结合精确准确性、排名恢复和有向错误分析的新评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机源于大语言模型价值评估的隐含前提——模型需能可靠识别具体情境中表达的价值，但此能力尚未系统验证，需填补评估空白。

Method: 采用受控top-1识别协议，基于Schwartz十项基本价值观；构建1,000条俄罗斯情境文本数据集（每项平衡十价值，双人独立标注）；评估21个指令调整LLM的20个可靠运行（形成语义面板），通过固定排名响应协议测试。

Result: Acc@1=0.683（Acc@3=0.892），表明模型常定位正确区域但排名不稳定；相邻价值导致50.9%语义错误（高于检查点特定null的24.4%）；八种有向混淆重复出现，含高度不对称模式（如Universalism→Benevolence）和双向边界（Stimulation-Hedonism），混淆严重性检查点特异且可能偏差高阶价值档案。

Conclusion: 结果证实价值识别需多维度评估：未来工作应整合精确准确性、排名恢复和有向错误分析，以解决模型混淆问题，避免高阶价值推断偏差。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated through the values they endorse, but such evaluations presuppose that models can identify the value expressed in a concrete situation. We study this prerequisite as controlled top-1 recognition over Schwartz's ten basic values. Our evaluation set contains 1,000 Russian situational texts, balanced across the ten values and independently labeled by two human annotators per item. We evaluate 21 instruction-tuned LLM runs under a fixed ranked-response protocol; 20 runs with reliable outputs form the semantic panel. Pooled Acc@1 is 0.683 and Acc@3 is 0.892, showing that models often locate the correct motivational region while ranking close alternatives unstably. Adjacent values account for 50.9% of semantic errors, compared with 24.4% under a checkpoint-specific null. Eight directed confusions recur across checkpoints and human-confirmed subsets. Several are strongly asymmetric, including Universalism to Benevolence, Tradition to Conformity, and Security to Power, whereas Stimulation-Hedonism forms a bidirectional boundary. Their severity is checkpoint-specific and can bias higher-order value profiles. The results motivate value-recognition evaluation that combines exact accuracy, ranked recovery, and directed error analysis.

</details>


### [113] [Sound Probabilistic Safety Bounds for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20286)
*Mahdi Nazeri,Anne-Kathrin Schmuck,Sadegh Soudjani,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出新框架计算LLM生成有害输出概率的严格界限，利用潜在空间特征优化搜索，实现高效可靠的危害概率下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以精确量化LLM在极低概率下产生有害内容的风险，缺乏数学上可靠的危害性评估机制，亟需可形式化证明的安全认证方法。

Method: 提出基于Clopper-Pearson置信区间的PAC界限计算框架，核心创新是设计利用潜在空间特征的分支优先级算法，优化自回归生成树的有害输出路径搜索。

Result: 实验验证该方法能在真实危害概率极低场景下高效计算非平凡下界，且所获下界经数学证明严格小于实际危害概率，对主流LLM取得显著成效。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了对LLM危害性的统计认证与可靠评估，为安全评估提供了新工具，使LLM的安全性分析具有数学保证。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for computing rigorous bounds on the probability that a large language model (LLM) generates harmful output to a given prompt. We study a new application of the Clopper-Pearson confidence intervals to obtain probably approximately correct (PAC) bounds for this problem. As our main technical contribution, we propose an algorithm that leverages features in the latent space to prioritize exploring branches in the auto-regressive generation tree that are more likely to produce harmful outputs. Our approach in particular enables the efficient computation of useful lower bounds, even in scenarios where the true harm probability is extremely small, and crucially, the obtained lower bounds are sound, i.e., formally proven to be less than the actual harmfulness probability: our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by computing non-trivial lower bounds on state-of-the-art LLMs. This study newly enables the evaluation and statistical certification of LLMs.

</details>


### [114] [PyroDash: Cost-Efficient Token-Level Small-Large Language Model Collaborative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20327)
*Niqi Lyu,Pengtao Shi,Wei Qiu,Jianlin Zhong,Sicong Xia,Jianyao Ma,Yicheng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PyroDash是一个成本感知的token级SLM-LLM协作推理框架，通过SLM动态请求LLM辅助，在数学推理任务中显著降低推理成本同时保持高准确率，例如在λ=0.6时成本从$49.36降至$1.78，准确率仍达54.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）推理能力强但服务成本高，小型语言模型（SLMs）成本较低但在复杂问题上可靠性不足，亟需一种平衡性能与成本的高效推理方案。

Method: PyroDash采用三阶段训练SLM：控制token嵌入学习、offloading导向监督微调、基于Group Relative Policy Optimization的成本感知对齐；推理时SLM通过控制token决定是否向LLM请求帮助，由Collaborate Engine单点移交查询和部分推理轨迹，无需额外路由器或LLM重训练。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准测试中，PyroDash实现灵活的准确性-成本权衡：λ=0.05时平均准确率达64.04%（较LLM-only基线提升6.36个百分点），成本降低20.4%；λ=0.6时准确率54.55%，LLM token占比仅1.90%，单次推理LLM调用0.012次，总成本从USD 49.36降至USD 1.78。

Conclusion: PyroDash证明基于学习的token级移交机制能有效减少LLM使用量，同时维持强大的推理性能，为成本敏感的AI推理场景提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide strong reasoning capabilities but are expensive to serve at scale, whereas small language models (SLMs) are cheaper but less reliable on difficult problems. We introduce PyroDash, a cost-aware framework for token-level SLM-LLM collaborative inference. During generation, the SLM decides whether to request assistance by emitting a control token. A Collaborate Engine then sends the query and partial reasoning trace to a frozen LLM for completion through a single handoff. The policy is internalized in the SLM, requiring neither a separate router, LLM retraining, nor access to LLM logits. PyroDash trains the SLM in three stages: control-token embedding learning, offloading-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and cost-aware alignment with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its reward balances answer accuracy against inference cost normalized by LLM-only inference. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, PyroDash supports different accuracy-cost operating points. With $λ=0.05$, it achieves 64.04 percent average accuracy, 6.36 percentage points above the LLM-only baseline, while reducing cost by 20.4 percent. With $λ=0.6$, it achieves 54.55 percent accuracy with a 1.90 percent LLM token ratio and 0.012 LLM calls per example, reducing total cost from USD 49.36 to USD 1.78. These results show that learned token-level handoffs can reduce LLM use while preserving strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [115] [Generative AI floods and dilutes the market for books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20349)
*Tuhin Chakrabarty,Xinyue Liu,Jane C. Ginsburg,Paramveer Dhillon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 生成式AI虽常被贬为低质内容，但研究显示其通过规模化生产正显著改变自出版小说市场结构：AI文本含量高的书籍虽销售占比小，却随时间扩大市场份额并抢占顶级排名，市场扩张速度远超收入增长，在Kindle Unlimited环境中尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验生成式AI产生的书籍是否真如普遍假设般缺乏商业价值（即被视为买家忽略的低质“垃圾”），从而验证其对创意市场的实际影响力。

Method: 对2023至2026年间亚马逊平台14,419本自出版类型小说进行全文本AI检测，识别AI文本含量（>25%视为实质含AI），并匹配至每日销售记录（截至2026年6月）；所有书籍均未主动披露AI内容使用情况。

Result: AI文本含量高的书籍占目录总量比例大但销售份额较小，却呈现持续增长的销售占比，并逐步取代无AI书籍占据稀缺的顶级销售排名；同期，有销售记录书籍数量激增19.2倍，而季度收入仅增8.9倍，导致多数类型中单本书收入下降；在AI扩散率高及Kindle Unlimited普及度高的类型中，无AI书籍损失最为严重；畅销书中，高AI含量书籍的语言独特性与现有书籍重叠度随收入上升，此趋势在无AI书籍中不存在。

Conclusion: 生成式AI主要通过规模效应而非内容质量重塑创意市场，其对销售结构和收益分配的影响直接关联到版权侵权合理使用辩护中的核心市场效果争议。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce book-length works of fiction at near-zero cost. These books are often dismissed as low-quality ``slop'' that buyers will ignore, and are assumed to carry little commercial weight. We test that assumption with full-text AI detection across 14,419 self-published genre-fiction books sold on Amazon from 2023 to 2026, matched to daily sales records through June 2026. None of these books disclose whether or not they contain AI-produced content. We find that books for which we detected substantial AI text ($>$ 25\%) make up a large share of the catalog but a smaller share of sales. Even so, they reach commercial scale, winning a growing share of sales over time and taking more of the scarce top-rank positions once held by books with no detected AI text. Over this period, the number of books with observed sales in a quarter grew 19.2-fold, while quarterly revenue grew only 8.9-fold. The market therefore added selling books faster than it added revenue, and revenue per selling book fell across most genres. Books with no AI text lose the most ground in genres with high AI diffusion, and most of all where Kindle Unlimited availability is high. Among top-selling books, those with substantial AI text draw on more distinctive language from existing books than do books with no AI text; for these books overlap rises with revenue, a gradient we do not detect for books with no AI text. Generative AI can thus reshape a creative market through scale rather than quality. Our results bear directly on the market-effect question at the center of the fair use defense to copyright infringement.

</details>


### [116] [Notes to Self: Can LLMs Benefit from Experiential Abstractions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20372)
*Chang Liu,Xinyu Li,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Humans distill experience into reusable abstractions, e.g., strategies and cautionary reminders, and apply them to gradually solve problems more effectively. We study whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly benefit from such experiential abstractions. From LLMs' solution traces on the MATH training set, a stronger teacher or the LLMs themselves extract natural-language abstractions into a retrievable library. We explore two usage modes: (1) inference-time retrieval and (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with abstraction-augmented training prompts. Experiential abstractions improve LLM performance on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks. Self-extracted abstractions match teacher-extracted ones, and our abstraction usage framework can transfer to other datasets and models. These findings suggest LLMs can extract and apply experiential abstractions much as humans leverage distilled experience.

</details>


### [117] [LKValues: Aligning Large Language Models with Sri Lankan Societal Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20410)
*Nethmi Muthugala,Supryadi,Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva,Ruijie Tao,Ovindu Gunatunga,Pengyun Zhu,Shaowei Zhang,Jingting Zheng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LKValues是首个针对斯里兰卡价值观对齐的资源套件，通过三语调查和新闻语料构建指令集与评估基准，微调改善了Qwen系列模型的文化价值对齐，减少跨语言差异，但效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）存在文化偏见，偏向西方规范，导致在多语言社会（如斯里兰卡）中处理本地价值观不当；现有基准忽略僧伽罗语等官方语言中的斯里兰卡特定价值观，阻碍文化敏感评估与微调。

Method: 从三语调查（205名受访者）中融合全球框架与LLM衍生本地构造，推导出40个多数支持的价值观；构建LKvaluesIT（150k僧伽罗语-英语新闻指令实例）和LKvaluesBench（1k评估实例）；评估专有与开放权重LLMs，并微调Qwen3.5-4B-Base、Qwen3.5-9B-Base和Aya-Expanse-8B-Base三个模型。

Result: 实验显示新LLMs仍存在低资源和跨文化价值对齐差距；LKValues微调显著提升Qwen系列模型在英语和僧伽罗语中的表现，减少无效输出和跨语言差异，但改善效果依赖模型家族。

Conclusion: LKValues有效嵌入斯里兰卡价值观，提供了可复制的管道用于低资源、国家特定多元文化价值对齐，数据集已公开可用。

Abstract: Value alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has been shown to be culturally biased toward Western norms. This results in the mishandling of local values in multilingual societies such as Sri Lanka that have their unique cultural dynamics. Existing benchmarks overlook Sri Lankan-contextualized values in its official language Sinhala, hindering culturally sensitive evaluation and fine-tuning. To bridge this gap, we propose LKValues, the first survey-grounded resource suite for Sri Lankan value alignment. From a trilingual survey of 205 respondents, blending adapted global frameworks and LLM-elicited local constructs, we derive 40 majority-endorsed societal values. Using these values, we construct LKvaluesIT, a Sinhala-English news-derived instruction corpus containing 150k scenario-based instances, and LKvaluesBench, a value-sensitive evaluation benchmark of 1,000 instances. We evaluate a set of proprietary and open-weight LLMs with LKvaluesBench. We fine-tune three open-weight base models (Qwen3.5-4B-Base, Qwen3.5-9B-Base, and Aya-Expanse-8B-Base). Our experiments show that newer and larger LLMs still exhibit low-resource and cultural value-alignment gaps. LKValues fine-tuning improves Qwen-family models in English and Sinhala, reducing invalid outputs and cross-lingual disparities, though gains remain model-family dependent. These highlight LKValues efficacy in embedding Sri Lankan values, offering a replicable pipeline for low-resource, country-specific pluralist value alignment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/NextME14/LKValues.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [ST-DDA: Dynamic Channel Estimation in the Doppler-Delay-Angle Domain via Sparse Subspace Tracking for TDD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19677)
*Xu Zhu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出ST-DDA方法，通过DDA域稀疏恢复和热启动策略，显著提升FH-TDD系统的信道估计精度，尤其在长探测间隔场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在频率跳变TDD系统中，全频段信道获取面临挑战：每个探测时隙仅观察有限频率子带，传统单时隙恢复无法充分利用历史观测数据。

Method: ST-DDA是一种在线稀疏子空间跟踪框架，在DDA域实现最新时隙重建：利用多普勒域能量集中特性进行窗口化稀疏恢复，并通过局部稳定性分析实现相邻时窗间的热启动；计算上采用交替子空间方法，结合位置编码卷积重加权以利用局部角度和多普勒结构。

Result: 实验显示，重加权的ST-DDA在重建精度和可靠性上优于动态压缩感知基线方法，尤其在较长探测间隔和较大频率跳变周期下，同时保持相当的每时隙运行时间。

Conclusion: ST-DDA通过有效利用历史观测和域转换，解决了FH-TDD系统的全频段信道获取难题，为相关应用提供了高效、准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate full-band channel acquisition in frequency-hopping time-division duplex (TDD) systems is challenging because each sounding slot observes only a limited frequency subband, while conventional single-slot recovery cannot fully exploit historical observations. We propose ST-DDA, an online sparse-subspace tracking framework for latest-slot reconstruction in the Doppler--delay--angle (DDA) domain. We first show that the Doppler-domain representation remains energy-concentrated under moderate channel variation, thereby supporting windowed DDA-domain sparse recovery. A local stability analysis further shows that the substantial overlap between adjacent windows enables the preceding-window estimate to warm-start each window-specific recovery problem, allowing the optimization progress to be carried across slots under a fixed per-slot iteration budget. For computational tractability, ST-DDA employs the alternating subspace method, which restricts the regularized least-squares fidelity updates to support-induced subspaces, together with position-encoded convolutional reweighting that exploits local angular and Doppler structures. Experiments show that reweighted ST-DDA achieves more accurate and reliable reconstruction than dynamic compressed-sensing baselines, particularly for longer sounding intervals and larger frequency-hopping periods, while maintaining comparable per-slot runtime.

</details>


### [119] [SpiRadar: Radar-Based Non-Contact Spirometry via Sparse Polynomial Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19798)
*Yonathan Eder,Yhonatan Kvich,Naama Golan,Adi Wagerhoff,Safit Levy,Lior Barbash,Ron Berant,Patrick Stafler,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: SpiRadar is a non-contact radar-based spirometry framework enabling accurate lung function assessment without mouthpieces, validated in pediatric cohorts with clinical-grade performance (RMSE 0.23L, BDR accuracy 89.5%) for asthma monitoring.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chronic respiratory diseases require spirometry as gold-standard assessment, but conventional methods face discomfort and technical barriers in pediatric populations due to mouthpiece reliance and cooperation challenges.

Method: Integrated radar framework with: (1) physiological preprocessing for signal extraction, (2) polynomial transformation linking thoracic displacement to volume curves, and (3) sparse optimization for calibration-free generalization; validated via subject-level cross-validation on 39 pediatric subjects (ages 6-18).

Result: Achieved clinical-grade performance: mean RMSE 0.23±0.12L for curve reconstruction, >0.84 correlations for key parameters, 89.5% BDR classification accuracy (sensitivity 90.9%, specificity 87.5%) on 58 trials including asthma patients.

Conclusion: Establishes clinical feasibility for non-contact spirometry, particularly valuable for pediatric asthma management and remote monitoring where physical contact limits conventional testing.

Abstract: Chronic respiratory diseases affect hundreds of millions of people worldwide, with spirometry serving as the gold standard for pulmonary function assessment. However, conventional spirometry's reliance on mouthpieces and nose clips creates discomfort and technical challenges that can compromise test quality, particularly in pediatric populations where cooperation difficulties are amplified. This study presents SpiRadar, a comprehensive radar-based framework for non-contact spirometry that eliminates physical contact requirements while enabling accurate curve reconstruction, clinical parameter estimation, and bronchodilator response (BDR) assessment. Our key contributions include: (1) a methodological framework integrating physiologically-motivated preprocessing for robust signal extraction, a feature-dependent polynomial transformation linking radar-measured thoracic displacement to spirometric volume curves, and sparse optimization enabling generalization to unseen subjects without individual calibration; (2) a rigorous validation on a pediatric cohort of 39 subjects (ages 6-18 years) undergoing 58 spirometry trials, including healthy children and asthma patients tested pre- and post-bronchodilator, using subject-level leave-one-out cross-validation; (3) clinical-grade performance outperforming alternative non-contact methods, achieving accurate curve reconstruction (mean RMSE: 0.23 +- 0.12 L) and strong correlations (above 0.84) for key spirometric parameters. BDR classification achieved 89.5% accuracy with balanced sensitivity (90.9%) and specificity (87.5%). These results establish clinical feasibility for our non-contact spirometry approach, with particular promise for pediatric asthma monitoring and remote patient care.

</details>


### [120] [WARA: A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Wireless Optimization Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19822)
*Yuan Guo,Yilong Chen,Chao Hu,Xianghao Yu,Liang Hong,Jie Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向无线领域的端到端自动研究框架WARA，基于大语言模型（LLM）多智能体系统，实现无线资源分配优化的全流程自动化。核心创新在于闭环工件控制系统，通过三阶段工作流（问题识别、建模实验、成果构建）和动态修复机制，显著提升研究质量，性能接近同行评审论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理在工具使用、代码执行等方面展现强大能力，为科学研究自动化开辟新途径；无线资源分配优化是表征系统性能极限和提升实际动态环境表现的关键领域，亟需高效自动化解决方案。

Method: 设计Wireless AutoResearch Agent (WARA) 闭环多智能体系统：1) 将研究流程分解为研究缺口识别与问题提出、优化建模与算法设计、研究交付构建三阶段；2) 采用工件介导过程，结构化上游输出驱动下游生成；3) 控制器管理门控验证工件一致性，失败时仅修复受影响部分；4) 集成LLM-based ScoringAgent评估研究有效性。

Result: 对比实验表明，WARA显著优于一次性LLM生成方法，研究质量接近近期已接收的同行评审论文；验证了闭环工件控制在LLM辅助无线优化研究中的高效性。

Conclusion: 证明了端到端LLM驱动的研究自动化可行性，凸显闭环工件控制在无线优化领域的实用价值，为AI辅助科研提供可复现框架（开源代码：https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown growing capabilities in tool use, code execution, artifact inspection, and iterative revision, creating new opportunities for automating scientific research. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first end-to-end autoresearch framework for the wireless domain, with a particular focus on wireless resource allocation optimization, an essential area for characterizing the fundamental performance limits of wireless systems and enhancing their practical performance under dynamic channel and network conditions. Specifically, we propose the Wireless AutoResearch Agent (WARA), a closed-loop multi-agent system that transforms an initial research topic into a complete research package. WARA organizes the research workflow into three phases: 1) research gap identification and problem proposal, 2) optimization modeling, algorithm design, and experimentation, and 3) research deliverable construction. Each phase follows an artifact-mediated process, in which structured upstream artifacts are consumed to generate downstream outputs. Controller-managed gates validate these artifacts and maintain consistency among problem formulations, algorithms, experiments, and research claims. When validation fails, WARA repairs only the affected artifact instead of restarting the entire workflow. We further design an LLM-based ScoringAgent to evaluate manuscript-level research validity. Comparative results show that WARA substantially outperforms one-shot LLM generation and approaches the quality profile of recently accepted peer-reviewed papers. These results demonstrate the potential of closed-loop artifact control for end-to-end LLM-assisted wireless optimization research. The source code is available at https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ

</details>


### [121] [Optimal Sensor Placement via Graph-constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19831)
*Feng Ji,Jingyang Dai,Wee Peng Tay,Sirajudeen Gulam Razul*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出连续空间传感器放置生成模型，利用flow matching技术生成最优传感器坐标，消除离散图组合优化的高计算成本，并在无线电传播模拟器上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图信号处理（GSP）传感器放置方法依赖离散图上的组合优化，导致计算开销大且传感器位置受限在图顶点；亟需一种高效、灵活的连续空间解决方案以支持实时部署。

Method: 将传感器放置重新公式化为连续空间生成问题：离线阶段，通过GSP优化生成最优配置样本，训练flow matching (FM)模型学习其分布；推理阶段，FM的速度场直接生成连续坐标；额外开发排列不变的有条件生成框架，适配固定锚点传感器场景。

Result: 在真实无线电传播模拟器实验中，该方法显著降低计算时间（相比传统离散优化），生成更优的连续传感器布局，并有效处理锚点约束，证明框架鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究创新性地将传感器放置转化为生成建模任务，提供高效、可扩展的连续空间解决方案，推动GSP在实际无线通信系统中的应用前景。

Abstract: Optimal sensor placement is a fundamental problem in graph signal processing (GSP), where a limited number of sensors are deployed to reconstruct a continuous signal field. Existing GSP methods rely on combinatorial optimization over discretized graphs, resulting in high computational cost and sensor locations restricted to graph vertices. We reformulate sensor placement as a continuous-space generative modeling problem. An offline GSP optimization routine first generates training samples of optimal sensor configurations, from which a flow matching (FM) model learns their distribution. At inference, the learned velocity field directly generates continuous sensor coordinates, eliminating online combinatorial optimization. We further develop a permutation-invariant conditional generation framework for deployment with fixed anchor sensors. Experiments on a realistic radio propagation simulator demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for sensor placement.

</details>


### [122] [A Covert Precision Satellite Communication Framework Assisted by Cooperative IRSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19883)
*Haoyang Wu,Yunfan Bai,Mei Shen,Yuwen Qian,Guangji Chen,Long Shi,Feng Shu,Jun Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于多智能反射面(IRS)合作的卫星隐蔽精确定向无线通信(CPWC)系统，通过OFDM随机子载波选择(RSCS)方法和交替优化-半定松弛(AO-SDR)算法实现高效隐蔽传输，检测错误概率方案显著优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星通信因广覆盖和高灵活性成为地面网络重要补充，但其开放链路易受窃听威胁，面临严峻安全挑战，需开发隐蔽通信方案保护敏感信息。

Method: 1. 设计多IRS协作的卫星CPWC系统架构；2. 开发OFDM-based RSCS技术集中信号能量；3. 基于相对熵和检测错误概率推导隐蔽性约束；4. 采用AO-SDR迭代算法求解相对熵约束下的最优波束成形；5. 利用SQP算法结合检测错误概率约束进一步细化方案。

Result: 成功推导封闭形式隐蔽性约束，获得高质量可行解；数值验证显示检测错误概率方案在隐蔽率上全面优于SOCP基准、随机相位设计、单比特IRS量化方案及无精确定向通信的SDR基线。

Conclusion: 所提CPWC系统有效解决了卫星通信隐蔽传输难题，通过IRS协作与先进信号处理技术实现安全高效的信息传递，为卫星通信安全提供新思路。

Abstract: Satellite communication (SatCom), as an effective complement to terrestrial networks, has attracted considerable attention from both academia and industry owing to its wide coverage and high flexibility. However, the inherent openness of satellite links renders them highly vulnerable to eavesdropping, thereby posing significant security challenges. In this paper, we propose a satellite covert precision wireless communication (CPWC) system, where multiple intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) cooperate to assist satellite transmissions, ensuring that confidential information is delivered to legitimate users while remaining undetectable to wardens. To further enhance covertness, an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based random subcarrier selection (RSCS) method is developed to concentrate the signal energy at the intended receiver. Under a practical satellite-terrestrial channel model, we derive closed-form covertness constraints for the CPWC system based on relative entropy and detection error probability. Under the relative-entropy constraint and the satellite power constraint, we maximize the covert rate by an alternating-optimization (AO) based semidefinite relaxation (SDR) iterative algorithm and obtain a high-quality feasible solution. Using this solution as a warm start, we further impose the detection-error-probability constraint and refine the beamformer through a sequential quadratic programming (SQP) based algorithm. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed CPWC system, where the detection-error-probability-based scheme outperforms the second-order cone programming (SOCP) benchmark, the random-phase-shift design, the one-bit IRS quantized scheme, and the SDR baseline without precise communication (PC) in terms of covert rate.

</details>


### [123] [Graph Distribution-valued Signals in Wasserstein Spaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20008)
*Yanan Zhao,Feng Ji,Xingchao Jian,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出图分布值信号（GDS）框架，解决经典图信号处理（GSP）对完整同步观测和严格时间对应的依赖问题，支持信号依赖图结构建模并捕获不确定性，严格推广传统图信号理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典GSP受限于必须获取所有顶点的同步观测数据及严格的输入-输出时间对应，难以处理现实应用中常见的信号依赖图结构（如动态网络拓扑变化）和固有不确定性，亟需新框架提升建模灵活性与鲁棒性。

Method: 将信号表示为Wasserstein空间中的概率测度（GDSs），建模图结构为信号实现的条件分布，建立基础GSP概念（如滤波器）与GDS对应关系，并推导GDS变换的理论连续性结果以确保输入扰动稳定性。

Result: 框架严格泛化传统图信号（视为Dirac delta测度），有效编码图拓扑不确定性；理论证明提供分布近似的稳定性保证；通过图滤波器学习和异常检测应用验证，实证显示在处理非同步数据和信号依赖图时优于经典方法。

Conclusion: GDS框架革新了图信号处理范式，突破经典局限，为动态不确定网络提供更严谨的数学基础，显著增强GSP在真实场景（如社交网络或传感器系统）中的适用性与可靠性。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for graph signal processing (GSP) in which signals are represented as graph distribution-valued signals (GDSs), i.e., probability measures in a Wasserstein space. This perspective addresses fundamental limitations of classical vector-based GSP, including the requirement for complete synchronous observations across vertices and the need for strict temporal correspondence in observed filter input--output pairs. Furthermore, by modeling the graph structure as a distribution conditioned on signal realizations, we provide a principled approach to signal-dependent graph structures, which are common in real-world applications, while explicitly encoding uncertainty in graph topology. Our framework inherently captures uncertainty and stochasticity while strictly generalizing traditional graph signals, which can be interpreted as Dirac delta measures. We develop a systematic correspondence between foundational GSP concepts and their GDS analogs, showing that classical formulations emerge as special cases of our framework. We establish theoretical continuity results for GDS transforms, providing stability guarantees for input perturbations and distribution approximations. We demonstrate the utility of this approach through example applications, including graph filter learning and anomaly detection, and validate its effectiveness through empirical studies.

</details>


### [124] [Drift-Aware RL-based Wavelet Denoising for Network-Traffic Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20011)
*Priyalakshmi Sheela,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出一种感知统计漂移的自适应小波去噪框架，通过任务导向优化提升网络监控中的异常检测与容量估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流量利用率测量受加性噪声和时间依赖统计漂移（如均值、方差、分布形状或尾部行为变化）干扰，导致传统静态小波去噪在漂移条件下失效，过度抑制有用结构，损害监控决策准确性。

Method: 设计漂移感知框架：以自适应小波去噪为预处理层，针对异常检测（恢复多尺度瞬态负载突发）和容量估计（恢复运营容量C95）优化；使用四检测器门控（Page-Hinkley、方差比、Jensen-Shannon、Anderson-Darling）判定漂移状态；PPO代理动态选择每窗口小波配置（离散-连续动作空间）；奖励基于下游任务效用而非重建保真度。

Result: 在多种漂移类型和输入信噪比（SNR）下基准测试，该方法优于低通移动平均滤波器、VisuShrink、SureShrink、BayesShrink及Wiener滤波器，在异常检测和C95估计任务中表现更佳。

Conclusion: 该框架有效应对统计漂移，通过任务导向优化显著提升网络监控可靠性，为动态环境下的预处理提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic-utilisation measurements for network monitoring are corrupted by additive noise and statistical drift: time-dependent change in the signal's mean, variance, distributional shape, or tail behaviour. Static wavelet denoising, calibrated under stationary independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) Gaussian assumptions, becomes mismatched under drift and, at moderate-to-high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), over-suppresses useful structure and degrades monitoring decisions. We propose a drift-aware framework treating adaptive wavelet denoising as a preprocessing layer optimised for two tasks: anomaly detection, recovering the multi-scale transient load bursts that noise and drift obscure, and capacity estimation, recovering the operational required capacity $C_{95}$ (95th percentile of utilisation). Because localised bursts are multi-scale structure a wavelet preserves but a low-pass filter removes, detection discriminates denoiser families. A four-detector gate (Page-Hinkley, variance-ratio, Jensen-Shannon, Anderson-Darling) determines when to invoke a learned policy, and a Proximal Policy Optimization agent selects a per-window wavelet configuration over a mixed discrete-continuous action space. Unlike prior work, the reward is downstream task utility, not reconstruction fidelity. The denoiser is benchmarked, per drift type and input SNR, against a low-pass moving-average filter, VisuShrink, SureShrink, BayesShrink, and a Wiener filter. Defining the anomaly target on the clean signal and the drift gate on the corruption keeps both stages non-circular.

</details>


### [125] [SNR-Dependent Mismatched Filtering for Bistatic OFDM Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20012)
*Ying Zhang,Fan Liu,Yifeng Xiong,Jie Yang,Xinyi Wang,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文分析了双静态OFDM-based ISAC系统中，解调错误导致测距性能受信噪比（SNR）影响的机制，并提供了理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于量化解调错误对测距旁瓣结构的SNR依赖性，以解决实际ISAC系统中不可避免的数据符号决策误差问题，从而优化低SNR条件下的测距可靠性。

Method: 方法包括在频率平坦衰落信道下，推导BPSK、QPSK和通用方形QAM星座的期望旁瓣水平（ESL）及平均主瓣水平的闭式表达式，并结合失配滤波模型分析时延域响应。

Result: 结果显示SNR驱动符号决策错误显著重塑旁瓣行为，理论推导通过仿真验证，准确揭示了ESL与SNR的定量关系。

Conclusion: 结论强调该分析为双静态OFDM-based ISAC系统的测距性能提供了SNR依赖的设计指导，尤其在低SNR场景下提升系统鲁棒性具有实践价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the ranging performance of a bistatic integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) system employing orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM), in which an ISAC transmitter emits a communication waveform carrying random data symbols, and a separate receiver performs ranging by correlating the received signal with a locally demodulated symbol sequence. Owing to inevitable demodulation errors, the ranging processor operates under mismatched filtering rather than ideal matched filtering, resulting in a delay-domain correlation response whose sidelobe structure explicitly depends on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Focusing on frequency-flat fading channels, we derive closed-form expressions for the expected sidelobe level (ESL) and the average mainlobe level of the resulting mismatched ranging response for BPSK, QPSK, and general square QAM constellations. The analysis quantitatively characterizes how SNR-driven symbol decision errors reshape the delay-domain sidelobe behavior, thereby providing analytical insight into the SNR-dependent scaling behavior of ranging performance in bistatic OFDM-based ISAC systems. Simulation results validate the theoretical derivations and confirm the accuracy of the proposed analysis.

</details>


### [126] [Uplink Positioning for PASS in Multipath Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20069)
*Yaoyu Zhang,Xin Sun,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出针对Pinching天线系统的上行多载波定位框架，包含基于矩阵铅笔和Rank-1的低复杂度测距算法及两阶段WNLS定位算法，理论分析与实验表明MP算法精度更高但计算量大，Rank-1算法复杂度低但存在误差底限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pinching天线系统需精准的上行定位以优化用户附近的无线传播，尤其在多径环境中保障通信效率，现有方法在多径干扰下性能受限，亟需高效测距与定位方案。

Method: 建立上行多载波定位框架：①MP测距算法利用Hankel矩阵移位不变性分离LOS/NLOS分量；②Rank-1测距算法通过截断SVD直接提取主导LOS延迟；③两阶段WNLS定位算法融合测距结果估计三维位置；并推导测距方差与位置误差界（PEB）进行理论分析。

Result: 数值结果：①MP算法精度和鲁棒性优于Rank-1，但计算复杂度更高；②MP定位误差严格遵循PEB趋势，而Rank-1因多径偏差产生误差底限；③MP定位精度随子载波数量增加显著提升。

Conclusion: MP算法在高精度需求场景（如子载波充足时）表现最佳，而Rank-1适合资源受限环境；研究揭示了误差传播机制，为PASS系统定位设计提供理论依据，平衡精度与复杂度的选择需依据实际部署条件。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) enhance wireless propagation by activating or placing pinching antennas (PAs) near users. Therefore, accurate uplink positioning is essential for efficient communication. In this paper, an uplink multi-carrier positioning framework is established for PASS in multipath environments. Matrix pencil (MP)-based and low-complexity Rank-1 ranging algorithms are proposed to estimate the distances between the PAs and the user. For the MP-based ranging algorithm, the line-of-sight (LoS) component is separated from non-line-of-sight components by exploiting the shift-invariance property of the Hankel matrix, thereby enabling accurate distance estimation. For the Rank-1 ranging algorithm, the dominant LoS delay is directly isolated through truncated singular value decomposition, thereby avoiding matrix inversions. Subsequently, a two-stage weighted nonlinear least-squares (WNLS) positioning algorithm is designed to estimate the three-dimensional user position. To gain further insights, a comprehensive theoretical performance analysis of the proposed ranging and positioning algorithms is conducted. The closed-form ranging variances and position error bound (PEB) are derived to reveal the error propagation mechanism. Numerical results demonstrate that: i) The MP-based algorithm achieves higher accuracy and robustness than the Rank-1-based algorithm, while the Rank-1-based algorithm has lower computational complexity. ii) The positioning error of the MP-based algorithm follows the same trend as the derived PEB, whereas the Rank-1 algorithm exhibits an error floor due to multipath bias. iii) The positioning accuracy of the MP algorithm improves as the number of subcarriers increases.

</details>


### [127] [Temporal Broadening-Aware Multiplexing for Joint Sensing and Communication in THz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20074)
*Saira Rafique,Ahmed Naeem,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出TBE感知复用框架，利用太赫兹传播特性，在无干扰下共存传感与通信，延迟降低66.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹波段在6G网络中支持高分辨率传感和高速通信，但分子吸收引起的时间扩展效应导致回波时间扩散，降低测距精度并需长保护间隔，造成延迟和时序资源浪费。

Method: 提出TBE感知复用框架，利用时间扩展的距离依赖性：在远端传感接收器预分配保护间隔中，机会性嵌入低功率单载波通信脉冲给近端用户，实现传感与通信脉冲的无干扰共存。

Result: 仿真显示，与功率域非正交多址接入（PD-NOMA）和固定保护间隔方案相比，所提方法误码率更低、传感精度更高，且延迟减少66.5%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效克服太赫兹传播中的时间扩展限制，提升频谱效率，为6G网络中传感与通信一体化提供关键技术支撑。

Abstract: High-resolution wireless sensing has become an integral component of futuristic 6G networks alongside high-rate communication. Terahertz (THz) band enables both functions through its extremely large bandwidth, providing sub-centimeter level sensing precision and multi-gigabit data rates. However, THz propagation suffers from severe channel impairments such as molecular absorption (MoA) and the resulting temporal broadening effect (TBE). For sensing, TBE causes temporal spreading of received echoes, leading to degraded range resolution and necessitating long guard intervals between consecutive sensing pulses to avoid overlap. These guards, while necessary for far sensing receiver (Rxsens), cause latency and inefficient temporal use. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a TBE-aware multiplexing framework that exploits the distance-dependent nature of TBE to enable interference-free coexistence of sensing and communication (S&C) pulses. A guard interval preallocated for the worst-case TBE at far Rxsens is opportunistically reused to embed a low-power single-carrier communication pulse for a nearby user experiencing minimal broadening. Limited TBE confines S&C pulses within their designated slots at short distances, while the broadened and attenuated communication pulse at the distant Rxsens becomes negligible, eliminating the need for successive interference cancellation (SIC). Simulation results reveal that compared with power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (PD-NOMA) and fixed-guard alternative, the proposed scheme achieves superior bit-error rate, sensing accuracy, and latency performance, with up to 66.5% latency reduction under heavy traffic.

</details>


### [128] [WiFi Sensing via Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20103)
*Heping Wang,Zhongqin Wang,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ReWiS框架，利用蓄水池计算处理WiFi感知中的时钟异步、跨域变化和部署后更新问题，在低计算预算下实现高效迁移学习和实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际WiFi感知需解决时钟异步、跨域差异及部署后更新需求，同时受限于AP、路由器和嵌入式IoT设备的计算资源，传统方法难以平衡性能与开销。

Method: 设计ReWiS框架：1) 将CSI转化为包含共性/天线特异性/差分运动特征的微多普勒流；2) 使用图耦合蓄水池编码时序信息；3) 部署后冻结蓄水池，仅用少量目标样本微调紧凑读出层。

Result: 在大规模WiFi感知基准上达到89.2%域内macro-F1和82.0%跨域均值macro-F1，仅使用0.72M参数读出层；轻量级部署后适应将性能提升至88.5%，优于同等协议下的深度基线（87.5%-89.2%），且优化成本和CPU延迟更低。

Conclusion: ReWiS提供了一种适用于可部署WiFi感知的实用蓄水池设计，具备低计算开销、强迁移能力和低功耗硬件实现潜力。

Abstract: Practical WiFi sensing must handle clock-asynchronous links, cross-domain variation, and post-deployment updating under the limited compute budget of access point (AP), router, and embedded Internet-of-Things platforms such as ESP-class devices. Reservoir computing (RC) is attractive in this setting because its temporal encoder can remain fixed while only a lightweight readout needs to be optimized and updated. To address these deployment challenges under tight compute budgets, we present ReWiS, a WiFi-sensing-oriented reservoir framework that transforms channel state information (CSI) into structured micro-Doppler streams with common, antenna-specific, and differential motion cues, encodes them with a graph-coupled reservoir, and adapts to a new domain after deployment by freezing the reservoir and fine-tuning only a compact readout with a few labeled target samples. On a large-scale WiFi sensing benchmark, ReWiS achieves 89.2% in-domain macro-F1 and 82.0% mean cross-domain macro-F1 with only a 0.72M trainable readout, improves to 88.5\% after lightweight post-deployment adaptation, and remains competitive with recent deep baselines evaluated under the same protocol, which achieve 87.5%-89.2% mean cross-domain macro-F1, while requiring lower optimization cost and lower CPU latency. These results indicate that ReWiS provides a practical reservoir-based design for deployable WiFi sensing, with further potential for low-power hardware realization.

</details>


### [129] [Ambiguity-Resolved Micro-Doppler Construction for Asynchronous Bistatic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20108)
*Yiyi Xu,Zhongqin Wang,Kai Wu,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种微多普勒构建框架，通过循环差分和多维滤波解决双静态ISAC中的时钟异步问题，显著提升了WiFi手势识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双静态部署中收发时钟异步导致信道状态信息随机相位波动，现有CSI比率净化和CACC方法存在非线性失真、镜像模糊及二阶副产物问题，限制了多目标可扩展性和运动多普勒特征提取。

Method: 提出微多普勒构建框架：首先用循环差分衰减主导镜像分量；然后利用残差项发生在差分延迟坐标且缺乏有序AoA导引结构的特点，设计轻量级延迟-AoA-多普勒管道隔离目标运动响应；最后聚合为高信噪比微多普勒表示。

Result: 在大规模WiFi手势数据集上，方法在四个域因素下泛化，准确率达90.1%-98.2%，比代表性基线方法平均提高约18个百分点；消融研究验证了残差抑制和多维滤波的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了双静态ISAC中的镜像模糊和残差干扰问题，显著提升了手势识别性能，证明了残差抑制和多维滤波在多目标可扩展微多普勒构建中的价值。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) can turn wireless networks into pervasive sensing platforms, but bistatic deployments are impaired by transceiver clock asynchrony, which induces random phase fluctuations in channel state information (CSI). CSI-ratio sanitization introduces nonlinear distortion that limits multi-target scalability and complicates delay- and angle-of-arrival (AoA)-domain processing. Cross-antenna conjugate multiplication (CACC) preserves a linear structure, but leaves mirror ambiguity and second-order by-products that corrupt motion-induced Doppler signatures. We develop a micro-Doppler construction framework that resolves the ambiguity and suppresses these residual by-products. Cyclic differencing first attenuates the dominant mirror component. We then exploit the facts that residual terms occur at differenced delay coordinates and lack an ordered AoA steering structure, designing a lightweight delay-AoA-Doppler pipeline that isolates the desired kinematic response without coherently accumulating residuals at target bins. The filtered responses are aggregated into a higher-SNR micro-Doppler representation. Ablation studies confirm the value of residual suppression and multidimensional filtering. On a large-scale WiFi gesture dataset, the proposed representation generalizes across four domain factors, attaining 90.1%-98.2% accuracy and outperforming representative baselines by about 18 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [130] [Swarm and Evolutionary Computation for Near-Field Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20139)
*Parisa Ramezani,Seyed Jalaleddin Mousavirad,Mattias O'Nils,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Near-field localization has attracted significant attention in recent years due to the move toward higher frequencies and extremely large aperture arrays, which expand the near-field region and bring many sources into it. This implies that antenna arrays can be used to localize not only in angle but also in range. Although a wide range of localization methods has been developed, each comes with limitations that may hinder practical deployment. This article focuses on a class of techniques that has received relatively little attention in the prior literature despite its strong potential for accurate and efficient location estimation: swarm and evolutionary computation (SEC). These methods are well-suited to the complex optimization landscape of near-field localization and can offer important advantages over conventional approaches such as grid-based subspace methods and deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [131] [ISAC-Assisted Channel Knowledge Map Generation for Physical Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20176)
*Luca Bonaventura,Edoardo Gardin,Alessia Barison,Francesco Ardizzon,Stefano Tomasin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 利用ISAC技术重建环境布局构建信道知识图（CKM），用于验证发射器位置，实现物理层认证（PLA），并评估误差影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对无线通信中的欺骗攻击，研究利用ISAC的环境感知能力，通过重建物体布局增强物理层认证的安全性，确保合法发射器身份验证。

Method: 首先，利用ISAC能力重建多接收器周围对象的布局；其次，应用射线追踪到重建环境推断传播信道，构建信道知识图（CKM）；接着，利用CKM比较接收信号估计信道与预期数据，验证合法发射器位置（结合近似已知位置）；最后，分析CKM重建错误和接收端信道估计噪声对PLA性能的影响。

Result: 评估了ISAC-induced CKM重建错误和接收端信道估计噪声对虚假报警概率和漏检概率的影响，揭示了这些误差因素对PLA性能的量化影响。

Conclusion: 提出的基于ISAC的PLA机制有效，能够利用环境重建实现可靠发射器认证，并通过文献ISAC数据集验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables the acquisition of environmental information by leveraging wireless signals transmitted for communication purposes. In this paper, we utilize this capability to reconstruct the layout of objects surrounding multiple receivers. Ray tracing is then applied to the reconstructed environment to infer the propagation channels for various transmitter positions, thereby constructing a channel knowledge map (CKM). The CKM is then used to verify the position of a legitimate transmitter, authenticating it against an adversarial device attempting to impersonate it from a different location. This physical layer authentication (PLA) mechanism utilizes the approximate known position of the legitimate transmitter, obtained, for instance, from the network as in cross-layer authentication, to compare the channel estimated from the received signal with the corresponding CKM data. We evaluate the impact on the PLA performance of both ISAC-induced CKM reconstruction errors and receiver-side channel estimation noise, in terms of false alarm and missed detection probabilities. Finally, the proposed approach is validated using an ISAC dataset from the literature.

</details>


### [132] [JEPA-CFM: A Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture-based Channel Foundation Model for Robust Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20202)
*Yuan Gao,Yiming Liu,Jun Jiang,Jianbo Du,Shunqing Zhang,Xiaoli Chu,Kai-Kit Wong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出JEPA-CFM解决流体天线系统中的CSI获取和外推问题，通过学习抽象结构显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统（FAS）作为6G关键技术，利用天线自由移动增强空间多样性，但实时CSI获取、信道值外推及用户定位受限于强空间相关性与数据稀疏性，亟需高效解决方案。

Method: 设计基于联合嵌入预测架构的通道基础模型（JEPA-CFM），预训练融合掩码自编码器重建损失、JEPA潜在预测损失及切片各向同性高斯正则化（SIGReg）；后接轻量化任务头（解码器用于信道外推，全局平均池化+MLP用于无线定位）。

Result: DeepMIMO城市环境仿真显示，JEPA-CFM在信道外推和无线定位任务上性能显著优于传统掩码自编码器基线，尤其在强空间相关和稀疏数据条件下鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: JEPA-CFM有效突破FAS核心瓶颈，通过抽象特征学习提升模型泛化能力，为6G无线网络提供创新且实用的技术路径。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) have emerged as a promising technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks. By allowing antenna elements to move freely within a compact region, FAS can exploit rich spatial diversity without additional hardware. However, acquiring real-time channel state information (CSI), extrapolating channel values to unmeasured antenna ports, and determining accurate user positions remain major obstacles. These challenges stem mainly from strong spatial correlations within the limited aperture and the scarcity of observable data. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces joint embedding predictive architecture (JEPA)-based channel foundation model (CFM) specifically designed for FAS. The model adopts JEPA to learn versatile representations by extracting high-level latent embeddings of masked or unobserved channel segments. Unlike conventional approaches that attempt pixel-by-pixel reconstruction of raw CSI coefficients, JEPA-CFM focuses on predicting abstract structures in a compact feature space. The pre-training objective combines three complementary loss terms: the standard masked autoencoder reconstruction loss, the JEPA latent prediction loss, and a sliced isotropic Gaussian regularization (SIGReg) term. Together, these components prevent representation collapse and significantly enhance robustness under severe spatial correlation and highly sparse observations. After pre-training, the encoder is frozen, and lightweight task-specific heads are attached: a decoder for channel extrapolation and a global average pooling layer followed by a multi-layer perceptron regression head for wireless positioning. Extensive simulations in the realistic DeepMIMO urban scenario demonstrate that JEPA-CFM substantially outperforms the conventional masked autoencoder baseline in channel extrapolation and wireless positioning.

</details>


### [133] [Joint Chirp Parameter Selection and Low-Complexity MMSE Receiver Design for AFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20227)
*Ruiyuan Mao,Qu Luo,Jianguo Li,Fabien Héliot,Tianqi Mao,Pei Xiao,Hee Wook Kim,Kai Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高移动性通信场景中的仿射频率分复用（AFDM），提出啁啾参数选择与低复杂度MMSE接收机的联合优化方法，利用有效信道矩阵结构特性，仿真验证了搜索效率提升和性能-复杂度权衡改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM作为应对双选择性信道的高移动性通信波形具有潜力，但需解决最优啁啾参数选择和低复杂度接收机设计问题，以实现低计算负担下的满意误码率（BER）性能。

Method: 基于离散仿射傅里叶变换（DAFT）的对角-循环结构派生简化BER指标，开发快速循环对角聚合（FCDA）算法优化c1参数；通过路径-wise结构化稀疏化构建循环带状有效信道矩阵，设计低复杂度带状MMSE（LC-BMMSE）接收机并采用带状Cholesky分解；进一步提出基于分层搜索的联合啁啾参数与结构化稀疏化（HS-JCPS）算法，实现复杂度约束下的参数联合优化。

Result: 仿真结果显示，FCDA算法将最优c1参数的搜索时间较传统BER基准降低一个数量级；HS-JCPS算法结合LC-BMMSE接收机可识别近最优c1参数，同时显著改善性能-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了AFDM系统的高复杂度挑战，通过结构特性利用实现了BER性能提升和计算效率优化，验证了联合优化的实际价值。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising waveform against doubly selective channels under high-mobility communication scenarios. Optimal chirp parameter selection and reduced-complexity receiver design in AFDM are essential for achieving satisfactory bit error rate (BER) performance with low computational complexity. In this paper, we investigate the joint optimization of chirp-parameter selection and low-complexity minimum mean square error (MMSE)-based receiver design by exploiting the structural characteristics of the AFDM effective channel matrix (ECM). First, a simplified BER performance metric is derived by leveraging the diagonal and circulant structure of the discrete affine Fourier transformation (DAFT), based on which a fast circulant-diagonal aggregation (FCDA) algorithm is developed for efficient $c_1$ selection. Then, a low-complexity banded MMSE (LC-BMMSE) receiver is developed by constructing a cyclic-banded ECM through path-wise structured sparsification, where banded Cholesky factorization is employed to avoid direct matrix inversion. Building upon the proposed BER metric and the LC-BMMSE receiver, a hierarchical-search-based joint chirp parameter and structured sparsification (HS-JCPS) algorithm is further proposed to jointly optimize the chirp parameter and sparsification pattern under a given complexity constraint. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed FCDA reduces the search time for the optimal $c_1$ by an order of magnitude compared with using a BER-based criterion. Moreover, the proposed HS-JCPS algorithm with the LC-BMMSE receiver can identify a near-optimal $c_1$, while attaining a superior performance-complexity tradeoff.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe is a real-world, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial platform, addressing limitations of existing coarse-grained traces. It enables fine-grained analysis of workload dynamics (arrival patterns and token behavior) across diverse models/tasks and provides a configurable workload generator for benchmarking multi-model serving systems, available on GitHub.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Efficient LLM serving under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world workloads, but existing studies rely on proxy traces or coarse characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model platforms, hindering optimization for low latency and high throughput.

Method: The authors present FineServe, an in-the-wild dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of workload dynamics. They analyze arrival dynamics and token behavior across heterogeneous models/tasks, then develop a workload generator to compose fine-grained, model-aware workload mixtures for configurable benchmarking of serving systems.

Result: Analysis reveals fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales, and task intents. The FineServe generator supports realistic, configurable workload compositions tailored for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in multi-model LLM serving systems.

Conclusion: FineServe establishes a realistic, fine-grained foundation for studying and optimizing LLM serving systems by exposing previously uncaptured workload dynamics, directly supporting the evaluation of critical system design choices.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [135] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [136] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenEvoShield，一个共进化持续防御框架，针对LLM多智能体系统中的动态攻击问题。通过解耦攻击与正常行为的学习速率、维护动态边界及多粒度检测，在100轮部署实验中有效检测新攻击并保持低误报率，显著优于静态和持续基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在安全关键应用部署中面临双重动态威胁：对手持续优化攻击策略，同时正常代理行为随系统扩展漂移。现有防御基于封闭世界假设，分布偏移时迅速失效，亟需能适应动态环境的持续防御机制。

Method: OpenEvoShield采用共进化框架，包含四个核心模块：(M1)非对称速率控制器，利用双漂移信号解耦攻击侧高速与正常侧低速学习；(M2)正常边界更新器，以慢速率维护动态行为边界；(M3)EWC正则化策略集成，实现快速适应无灾难性遗忘；(M4)能量基多粒度检测器，融合节点、子图及图级证据分类分布外攻击。

Result: 在五个基准测试和四种MAS拓扑的100轮部署实验中，OpenEvoShield检测率显著提升，成功识别多数未见攻击（如注入式恶意指令），误报率低于5%，全面超越静态防御和持续学习基线模型。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共进化机制有效应对LLM多智能体系统的动态威胁，提供了一种可扩展、低误报的持续防御方案。该框架突破封闭世界局限，为安全关键应用部署奠定鲁棒基础，未来可整合更多环境交互以增强适应性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [137] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了在GPU加速的大语言模型推理中启用机密计算的性能影响，发现机密模式导致首token时间（TTFT）平均增加21.8%-27.8%，全局吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，且大模型更早达到饱和临界点，需通过容量规划补偿性能损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI推理任务涉及敏感数据处理和专有模型保护需求的增长，机密计算成为实际部署的关键要求。然而，其引入的硬件加密机制可能显著影响GPU加速的大语言模型服务性能，需量化评估不同工作负载下的性能损耗及操作影响。

Method: 在单个NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU（部署于Intel TDX机密实例）上开展基准测试，对比非机密与机密执行模式对Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个模型的影响。测试指标包括TTFT、端到端延迟、单请求token生成吞吐量、全局token吞吐量及闭环并发请求吞吐量，覆盖固定请求率与动态并发场景。

Result: 在固定请求率实验中，机密模式使TTFT分别增加21.8%（Mistral-7B）和27.8%（Qwen3-30B-A3B），全局吞吐量下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发测试显示吞吐量差距维持在11.5%-20.2%，但大模型（Qwen3）在机密模式下更早达到性能饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理可维持可用吞吐能力，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳定的吞吐量惩罚效应和大模型特有的早饱和现象，需针对性优化资源配置。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [138] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FORMULASPIN是一个自博弈框架，通过利用公式执行的二元反馈解决梯度矛盾问题，在NL2FORMULA数据集上实现74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率，达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公式生成方法依赖静态监督数据，容易饱和，而电子表格公式编写仍是全球数亿用户的主要障碍。

Method: 引入FORMULASPIN自博弈框架，将训练建模为两人游戏；利用二元可执行性提供隐式监督，区分语义错误与有效风格变体，形成从语义正确性到风格精细化的自适应课程；并集成ExecVote语义级投票机制处理多个有效公式。

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到SOTA：NL2FORMULA上74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率，与使用额外偏好注释的模型相当，且优于传统SFT和商业专有模型。

Conclusion: 研究突显了自博弈在数据稀缺任务中的潜力，并展示了将其扩展至可执行领域之外的可行性。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [139] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在使用外部知识时无法有效辨别信息可靠性（来源辨别）和真理一致性（真理辨别），实验显示模型在两项能力上接近随机表现，过度依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，且更新不区分主张是否改善真理；尽管更大模型改善了真理辨别，但来源辨别仍是盲点，简单推理干预可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM与外部知识源（如互联网）集成时是否适当权衡信息：具体评估其对可靠来源的更新倾向（来源辨别）和当主张使先验更接近真实情况时的更新强度（真理辨别），以解决LLM在实际应用中信息处理可靠性的关键问题。

Method: 提出Learn2Discern (L2D) 实验框架和基准，基于三个规范公理（如信任可靠来源、优先更新真理一致性主张）及可解释指标；通过预注册、配额匹配的用户研究（n=299）验证公理有效性，并在13个模型上执行近67万试验测试信息辨别能力。

Result: 模型在来源和真理辨别上表现接近随机水平；依赖来源流行度是可靠性的两倍；更新幅度不区分主张改善或恶化相对于真实情况；模型在自身先验已准确的数据集上整合外部知识最有效；更新和更大模型改善了真理辨别但未改善来源辨别；简单推理时间干预（如提示调整）显著提升两种辨别能力。

Conclusion: LLM存在显著的信息辨别缺陷，尤其是来源辨别问题无法通过模型规模或复杂性解决，构成核心对齐挑战；发布的数据集和用户调查作为测试床，突显了随着LLM取代传统搜索，信息辨别能力对AI可靠性和用户信任的关键重要性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [140] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一种高效安全监控器，通过融合规则引擎与机器学习模型，显著提升LLM代理的运行时干预准确性（F1=0.949），同时保持超低延迟（0.205ms）和最小开销（<0.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具驱动的大型语言模型（LLM）代理正频繁执行高影响力操作，亟需可靠的运行时安全监控机制，以防范潜在风险并保障系统稳定性。

Method: NEXUS采用结构化计划框架，结合确定性安全规则、论证级检查及校准逻辑回归风险评分，实现四级干预策略（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）的精确路由。

Result: 在128实例合成基准上达F1 0.949及干预准确率0.6406，较纯规则系统提升27.3个百分点；在R-Judge（F1 0.861）、IPI（0% ASR）等测试集中表现优异，且在NEXUS-Stress基准下仍保持F1 0.881，凸显细粒度路由挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了混合方法在安全监控中的有效性，公开代码与基准为社区提供实用工具，同时指出细粒度干预策略设计的复杂性与未来优化方向。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [141] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级推理时解码层，通过在线约束优化方法实现生成式推荐系统的多目标slate生成，无需修改底层模型，同时平衡推荐相关性与辅助约束目标，并在真实系统中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统中多目标slate生成方法存在局限性：后处理技术未考虑生成式推荐的序列特性，而在训练中直接集成辅助目标则在大规模系统中难以实用。需要一种既能保留生成式推荐优势，又能有效处理多目标约束的新方法。

Method: 提出一种推理时解码层，将解码过程建模为在线约束优化问题，采用随机原对偶近似方案，在顺序选择项目过程中根据剩余约束松弛度动态调整相关性与辅助目标之间的权衡，无需修改或重新训练底层生成式推荐模型。

Result: 在离线实验和真实世界大规模在线A/B测试中，该方法显著改善了多目标权衡效果，辅助目标提升1.8%的同时保持用户满意度不受影响，并提供了约束违反和遗憾的理论保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的推理时解码框架提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案，能够在不改造成熟生成式推荐模型的前提下，有效支持多目标slate生成，平衡了推荐相关性与业务约束，具有良好的理论保障和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [142] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI
